# SSR throughput v2 — complete metric ledger

Raw captures referenced in this note are local experiment artifacts and are excluded from Git.
Published chart data is retained under `apps/docs/src/data`.

Current values only. Cells are mean / P50 / P75 / P95 / P99 where a mean exists, otherwise P50 / P75 / P95 / P99. Aggregate RPS is one elapsed-weighted lane observation; window-rate percentiles remain separate. Earlier v1 rates are not a runtime before/after comparison.
